2026-10-24T00:54:47.4879451Z # Run a local bridge, web server, and browser for a particular Cockpit page.
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4880474Z # This is useful for integration into .desktop files, for systems which don't
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4881543Z # have cockpit.socket enabled. The web server and browser run in an unshared
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4883840Z # network namespace, and thus are totally isolated from everything else.
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4884665Z #
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4885102Z # Usage:
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4885777Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 0
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4886786Z # For testing purposes one can also make cockpit-ws bind to all addresses
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4887692Z # (this is insecure and should not be used in prodcuction):
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4888598Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 1
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4889382Z #
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4889804Z set -eu
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4890119Z
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4890398Z THEME_ID="default"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4890891Z WEBUI_REMOTE=0
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4891376Z while getopts t:r: option
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4891888Z do
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4892327Z     case "${option}"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4892806Z         in
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4893656Z         t)THEME_ID=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4894251Z         r)WEBUI_REMOTE=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4894924Z         *) echo "Usage: $0 [-t THEME_ID] [-r WEBUI_REMOTE]" >&2
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4895613Z        exit 1 ;;
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4896096Z     esac
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4896535Z done
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4896847Z
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4897494Z URL_PATH="/cockpit/@localhost/anaconda-webui/index.html"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4898053Z
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4898353Z WEBUI_ADDRESS="127.0.0.1"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4898897Z if [[ "$WEBUI_REMOTE" == "1" ]]
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4899422Z then
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4899880Z     WEBUI_ADDRESS="0.0.0.0"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4900405Z fi
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4900710Z
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4901135Z echo "WEBUI_ADDRESS=$WEBUI_ADDRESS" > /tmp/webui-cockpit-ws.env
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4901893Z systemctl start webui-cockpit-ws

2026-10-24T00:54:47.4902672Z # Function to setup user-related environment
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4903903Z setup_user_environment() {
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4904612Z     # PKEXEC_UID is the uid for "gnome-initial-setup" or "liveuser"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4905424Z     # depending on how the installer gets started.
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4906109Z     #
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4906794Z     # It's unset on non-live-images, so we just use the current user then (root)
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4907663Z     if [ -n "${PKEXEC_UID:-}" ]; then
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4908386Z       # If PKEXEC_UID is set, use the user associated with it
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4909179Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(id -n -u "${PKEXEC_UID}")
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4909846Z     else
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4910776Z       # Try to find another user who is not root and has /usr/bin/bash as their shell
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4912036Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(grep '/usr/bin/bash' /etc/passwd | grep -v '^root' | cut -d: -f1 | head -n 1)
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4912769Z
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4913538Z       # If no such user is found, use the current user running the script
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4914338Z       if [ -z "${INSTALLER_USER:-}" ]; then
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4921233Z         INSTALLER_USER=$(id -un)
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4922271Z       fi
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4922745Z     fi
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4923368Z
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4923717Z     # Get the home directory of the selected user
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4924503Z     HOME_DIR=$(grep "^$INSTALLER_USER:" /etc/passwd | cut -d: -f6)
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4925211Z
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4925609Z     # Check if the home directory exists, otherwise create it
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4926368Z     if [ ! -d "$HOME_DIR" ]; then
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4927193Z       echo "The home directory $HOME_DIR does not exist. Creating it." >&2
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4928024Z       mkdir -p "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4928665Z       chown "$INSTALLER_USER" "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4929329Z     else
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4929929Z       echo "Home directory $HOME_DIR already exists." >&2
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4931841Z     fi
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4932168Z
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4932545Z     # Return the user and home directory for further use
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4933672Z     echo "$INSTALLER_USER"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4934353Z }

2026-10-24T00:54:47.4935023Z get_default_browser() {
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4935973Z     # Read the default browser from the anaconda runtime configuration file but wait till it's available
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4937233Z     # FIXME: Related to: https://issues.redhat.com/browse/INSTALLER-4223
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4938073Z     timeout=60
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4938609Z     elapsed=0
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4938971Z
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4939328Z     while [ ! -f /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf ]; do
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4940057Z         if [ "$elapsed" -ge "$timeout" ]; then
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4940848Z             echo "Timed out waiting for anaconda.conf to be available." >&2
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4941661Z             exit 1
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4942185Z         fi
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4942661Z         sleep 1
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4943436Z         elapsed=$((elapsed + 1))
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4944043Z     done
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4944398Z
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4944996Z     # FIXME: Should be provided as an argument to this script instead of grepping the config file
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4946216Z     DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(grep '^webui_web_engine =' /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf | cut -d'=' -f2)
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4947144Z     if [ -n "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" ]; then
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4948166Z         echo "Default browser set in anaconda.conf: $DEFAULT_BROWSER" >&2
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4949225Z         DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" | sed 's/^[[:space:]]*//;s/[[:space:]]*$//')
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4951245Z         echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4951833Z     else
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4952471Z         echo "No default browser set in anaconda.conf, using firefox." >&2
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4953623Z         echo "firefox"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4954140Z     fi
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4954589Z }

2026-10-24T00:54:47.4957520Z     FIREFOX_THEME_DIR="/usr/share/anaconda/firefox-theme"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4958372Z     FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH="${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR}/anaconda/firefox-profile"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4958999Z
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4959372Z     # make sure the profile directory exists and is empty
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4960061Z     if [ -d ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH} ]
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4960640Z     then
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4961505Z         echo "Cleaning up existing Anaconda Firefox profile directory." >&2
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4962313Z         rm -rf ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4963304Z     fi
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4963991Z     pkexec --user "${INSTALLER_USER}" mkdir -p ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4964644Z
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4965076Z     # populate the profile directory with our custom Firefox theme
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4965938Z     # - theme id is passed as the second argument of this script
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4966691Z     THEME_PATH="${FIREFOX_THEME_DIR}/${THEME_ID}"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4967191Z
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4967697Z     pkexec --user "${INSTALLER_USER}" cp -a "${THEME_PATH}/." ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4968401Z
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4968713Z     # set environment for Firefox
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4969611Z     BROWSER_ENV=(MOZ_APP_TITLE="" MOZ_APP_REMOTINGNAME="liveinst" MOZ_GTK_TITLEBAR_DECORATION=client)
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4970404Z
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4971099Z     BROWSER=(/usr/bin/env "${BROWSER_ENV[@]}" /usr/bin/firefox --new-instance --profile "${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}")
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4972006Z
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4972309Z     declare -p BROWSER
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4972858Z }
2026-10-24T00:54:47.4973514Z

2026-10-24T00:54:47.4989754Z

2026-10-24T00:54:47.5018917Z # Check if gettext exists
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5019347Z if ! type gettext > /dev/null 2>&1; then
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5019960Z   # If not, create a dummy function that returns the input verbatim

2026-10-24T00:54:47.5021184Z
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5021465Z # returns OK if $1 contains literal string $2 (and isn't empty)
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5022024Z strstr() {

2026-10-24T00:54:47.5022703Z }

2026-10-24T00:54:47.5023382Z # Run some action. Log its output.
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5023808Z action() {
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5024118Z     local STRING rc
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5024326Z
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5024458Z     STRING=$1
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5024798Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5025235Z     shift
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5025530Z     if "$@"; then
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5025883Z         success "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5026313Z     else
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5026634Z         failure "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5027057Z     fi
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5027343Z     rc=$?
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5027635Z     printf '\n'
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5027963Z     return $rc
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5028282Z }
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5028439Z
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5028709Z # verbose ->> very (very!) old bootup look (prior to RHL-6.0?)
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5029292Z # color ->> default bootup look
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5029847Z # other ->> default bootup look without ANSI colors or positioning
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5030443Z BOOTUP=color
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5030793Z # Column to start "[  OK  ]" label in:
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5031223Z RES_COL=60
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5031574Z # terminal sequence to move to that column:
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5032056Z MOVE_TO_COL="printf \\033[${RES_COL}G"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5032632Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'success' (bright green):
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5034686Z SETCOLOR_SUCCESS="printf \\033[1;32m"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5035281Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'failure' (bright red):
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5035890Z SETCOLOR_FAILURE="printf \\033[1;31m"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5036470Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'warning' (bright yellow):
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5037274Z SETCOLOR_WARNING="printf \\033[1;33m"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5037775Z # Terminal sequence to reset to the default color:
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5038303Z SETCOLOR_NORMAL="printf \\033[0;39m"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5038601Z
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5038767Z # NOTE: /dev/ttyS* is serial console.
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5039355Z # 'not a tty' is reported when executed under systemd service units
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5040009Z # where /dev/null is associated with standard input
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5040378Z
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5040533Z test_tty=$(LC_ALL=C tty)
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5040905Z case "$test_tty" in
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5041253Z     /dev/ttyS* | 'not a tty')
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5041642Z         BOOTUP=serial
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5041994Z         MOVE_TO_COL=
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5042387Z         SETCOLOR_SUCCESS=
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5042785Z         SETCOLOR_FAILURE=
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5043381Z         SETCOLOR_WARNING=
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5043777Z         SETCOLOR_NORMAL=
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5044131Z         ;;
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5044434Z esac
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5044627Z
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5044799Z # Log that something succeeded
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5045194Z success() {
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5045560Z     [ "$BOOTUP" != "verbose" ] && echo_success
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5046276Z     return 0
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5046557Z }
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5046707Z
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5046842Z # Log that something failed
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5047196Z failure() {
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5047492Z     local rc=$?
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5047872Z     [ "$BOOTUP" != "verbose" ] && echo_failure
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5048448Z     [ -x /bin/plymouth ] && /bin/plymouth --details
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5048971Z     return $rc
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5049301Z }
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5049476Z
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5049627Z echo_success() {
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5050021Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $MOVE_TO_COL
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5050507Z     printf '%s' "["

2026-10-24T00:54:47.5060064Z
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5060182Z fscheck() {
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5060510Z     local fstype="$1"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5060849Z     local fs="$2"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5061173Z     local d="${3:-$2}"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5061516Z     umount "$fs" >/dev/null 2>&1 || :
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5061934Z     case "$fstype" in
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5062307Z         ext[432])
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5062654Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5063386Z             flock "$d" e2fsck -yfv "$fs" || :
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5063852Z             ;;
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5064167Z         vfat|msdos)
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5064502Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5064882Z             flock "$d" fsck.fat -avVw "$fs"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5065320Z             ;;
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5065637Z         btrfs)
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5066021Z             printf '# btrfs check -p --repair --force'
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5067271Z             flock "$d" btrfs check -p --repair --force "$fs"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5068096Z             ;;
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5069131Z         xfs)
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5069983Z             printf '\n# xfs_repair -v'
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5070482Z             flock "$d" xfs_repair -v "$fs"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5071133Z             ;;
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5071492Z         f2fs)
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5071837Z             printf '# fsck.f2fs -afy'
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5072294Z             flock "$d" fsck.f2fs -afy "$fs"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5072746Z             ;;
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5073290Z         *)
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5073762Z             printf "\n\tERROR:   Filesystem checking for '%s' is not\n \
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5074393Z                 available.\n" "$fstype"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5074856Z     esac
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5075163Z }

2026-10-24T00:54:47.5124626Z mountPersistentHome() {
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5124865Z   # support label/uuid
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5125106Z   if [ "${homedev##LABEL=}" != "$homedev" ] ||
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5125408Z     [ "${homedev##UUID=}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5125708Z     homedev=$(blkid -o device -t "$homedev")
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5125970Z   fi
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5126064Z
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5126221Z   # if we're given a file rather than a blockdev, loop mount it
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5126567Z   if [ "${homedev##mtd}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5127207Z     # mtd devs don't have a block device but get magic-mounted with -t jffs2
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5127875Z     mountopts="-t jffs2"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5128280Z   elif [ ! -b "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5128794Z     if [ "${homedev##/run/initramfs/live}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5129544Z       action "Remounting live store r/w" mount -o remount,rw /run/initramfs/live
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5130186Z     fi
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5130529Z     homedev=$(losetup -f --show "$homedev")
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5130965Z   fi
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5131355Z   homefs=$(blkid -s TYPE -o value "$homedev" 2>/dev/null)
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5131735Z
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5131916Z   # if it's encrypted, we need to unlock it
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5132398Z   if [ "$homefs" = "crypto_LUKS" ]; then
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5133319Z     echo
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5133970Z     echo "Setting up encrypted /home device"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5134680Z     plymouth ask-for-password --command="cryptsetup luksOpen $homedev EncHome"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5135674Z     homedev=/dev/mapper/EncHome
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5136060Z   fi
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5136562Z   if [ "$homedev" ] && [ "${cmdline##* rd.skipfsck[= ]}" = "$cmdline" ]; then
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5137319Z     action "Checking home filesystem" fscheck "$homefs" "$homedev"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5137873Z   fi
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5137977Z
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5138073Z   # and finally do the mount
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5138356Z   mount ${mountopts:+$mountopts} "$homedev" /home
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5138741Z   # if we have /home under what's passed for persistent home, then
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5139172Z   # we should make that the real /home.  useful for mtd device on olpc
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5139567Z   [ -d /home/home ] && mount --bind /home/home /home
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5139901Z   [ -x /sbin/restorecon ] && /sbin/restorecon /home
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5140212Z   [ -d /home/liveuser ] && USERADDARGS="-M"
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5140469Z }
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5140570Z
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5140674Z cut=${cmdline##* persistenthome[= ]}
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5140939Z if [ ${#cut} -ne ${#cmdline} ]; then
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5141179Z   homedev=${cut%% *}
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5141454Z elif [ -e /run/initramfs/live/"${livedir}"/home.img ]; then
2026-10-24T00:54:47.5141957Z   homedev=/run/initramfs/live/"${livedir}"/home.img
